SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  -             "text": "### Microsoft Agent Framework code samples (Python)\r\n\r\n#### Quick Start\r\nConnect GitHub model first, then create the agent instance:\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom agent_framework import ChatAgent\r\nfrom agent_framework.openai import OpenAIChatClient\r\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start() -> None:\r\n    openaiClient=AsyncOpenAI(\r\n        base_url=\"https://models.github.ai/inference\",\r\n        api_key=\"<GITHUB_TOKEN>\",\r\n    )\r\n    chatClient=OpenAIChatClient(\r\n        async_client=openaiClient,\r\n        ai_model_id=\"<model-id>\"\r\n    )\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n    )\r\n    \r\n    result=await agent.run(\"hello\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # Hello! How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

Create the joke-telling agent\\n            self.agent = ChatAgent(\\n                chat_client=chat_client,\\n                name=\\\"JokeTeller\\\",\\n                instructions=\\\"\\\"\\\"You are a witty and family-friendly comedian AI. \\n                Your job is to tell creative, clean, and funny jokes about any topic the user provides.\\n                \\n                Guidelines:\\n                - Keep jokes appropriate for all audiences\\n                - Be creative and original with your humor\\n                - If asked about a topic, create 1-3 jokes related to that topic\\n                - Use various joke formats: puns, one-liners, knock-knock jokes, etc.\\n                - Make the jokes engaging and entertaining\\n                - If the topic is sensitive, redirect to light-hearted aspects\\n                \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n            )\\n            \\n            print(\\\"🎭 AI Joke Teller initialized successfully!\\\")\\n            print(\\\"💡 Using model:\\\", self.model_id)\\n            \\n        except Exception as e:\\n            print(f\\\"❌ Error initializing joke teller: {e}\\\")\\n            raise\\n    \\n    async def tell_joke(self, topic: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Generate and return a joke about the given topic.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            topic: The topic to create jokes about\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            The generated joke(s) as a string\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not self.agent:\\n            raise RuntimeError(\\\"Agent not initialized. Call initialize() first.\\\")\\n        \\n        try:\\n            # Create a prompt for joke generation\\n            prompt = f\\\"Tell me a funny, clean joke about: {topic}\\\"\\n            \\n            # Get response from the agent\\n            result = await self.agent.run(prompt)\\n            return result.text\\n            \\n        except Exception as e:\\n            return f\\\"😅 Oops! I couldn't come up with a joke right now. Error: {e}\\\"\\n    \\n    async def interactive_mode(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run the joke teller in interactive mode.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        print(\\\"\\\\n🎪 Welcome to the AI Joke Teller!\\\")\\n        print(\\\"📝 Enter any topic and I'll tell you a joke about it.\\\")\\n        print(\\\"💬 Type 'quit', 'exit', or 'bye' to stop.\\\\n\\\")\\n        \\n        while True:\\n            try:\\n                # Get topic from user\\n                topic = input(\\\"🎯 What topic would you like a joke about? \\\").strip()\\n                \\n                # Check for exit commands\\n                if topic.lower() in ['quit', 'exit', 'bye', '']:\\n                    print(\\\"👋 Thanks for using AI Joke Teller! Have a great day!\\\")\\n                    break\\n                \\n                # Generate and display joke\\n                print(\\\"🤔 Thinking of a joke...\\\")\\n                joke = await self.tell_joke(topic)\\n                print(f\\\"\\\\n😂 Here's your joke:\\\\n{joke}\\\\n\\\")\\n                print(\\\"-\\\" * 50)\\n                \\n            except KeyboardInterrupt:\\n                print(\\\"\\\\n\\\\n👋 Goodbye! Thanks for using AI Joke Teller!\\\")\\n                break\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                print(f\\\"❌ An error occurred: {e}\\\")\\n\\n\\nasync def main():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Main function to run the joke teller application.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    print(\\\"🎭 AI Joke Teller - Powered by Microsoft Agent Framework & GitHub Models\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 70)\\n    \\n    # Get GitHub token from environment variable or user input\\n    github_token = os.getenv('GITHUB_TOKEN')\\n    \\n    if not github_token:\\n        print(\\\"\\\\n🔑 GitHub Personal Access Token required to access models.\\\")\\n        print(\\\"💡 You can either:\\\")\\n        print(\\\"   1. Exiting.\\\")\\n            sys.exit(1)\\n    \\n    # Allow user to choose model (optional)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n🤖 Available models (or press Enter for default):\\\")\\n    print(\\\"   • openai/gpt-4.1-mini (default - fast & cost-effective)\\\")\\n    print(\\\"   • openai/gpt-4.1 (more capable)\\\")\\n    print(\\\"   • openai/gpt-4o-mini (multimodal)\\\")\\n    \\n    model_choice = input(\\\"\\\\n🎯 Choose model (or press Enter for default): \\\").strip()\\n    model_id = model_choice if model_choice else \\\"openai/gpt-4.1-mini\\\"\\n    \\n    try:\\n        # Create and initialize joke teller\\n        joke_teller = JokeTeller(github_token, model_id)\\n        await joke_teller.initialize()\\n        \\n        # Check if running in interactive mode or with command line argument\\n        if len(sys.argv) > 1:\\n            # Single joke mode - topic provided as command line argument\\n            topic = ' '.join(sys.argv[1:])\\n            print(f\\\"\\\\n🎯 Topic: {topic}\\\")\\n            joke = await joke_teller.tell_joke(topic)\\n            print(f\\\"\\\\n😂 Your joke:\\\\n{joke}\\\")\\n        else:\\n            # Interactive mode\\n            await joke_teller.interactive_mode()\\n            \\n    except Exception as e:\\n        print(f\\\"❌ Error: {e}\\\")\\n        sys.exit(1)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    # Run the async main function\\n    asyncio.run(main())\", \"filePath\": \"/C:/Users/dol/workspace/code/vscode-copilot-chat-sbh/test/scenarios/test-agent-code-gen/results/workspace0/joke_teller.py\"}",

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"

SDK\r\n- Use **Microsoft Agent Framework** for agent app, with following highlights\r\n  - Flexible Agent Framework: build, orchestrate, and deploy AI agents and multi-agent systems\r\n  - Multi-Agent Orchestration: Group chat, sequential, concurrent, and handoff patterns\r\n  - Plugin Ecosystem: Extend with native functions, OpenAPI, Model Context Protocol (MCP), and more\r\n  - LLM Support: OpenAI, Azure OpenAI, Azure AI Foundry, and more\r\n  - Runtime Support: In-process and distributed agent execution\r\n  - How can I assist you today?\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Add tool\r\nTools (or Function Callings) can let Agent interact with external APIs or services, enhancing its capabilities.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nfrom random import randint\r\nfrom typing import Annotated\r\n\r\n# Define tool(s) and add to 'ChatAgent'\r\ndef get_weather(\r\n    location: Annotated[str, \"The location to get the weather for.\"],\r\n) -> str:\r\n    \"\"\"Get the weather for a given location.\"\"\"\r\n    conditions = [\"sunny\", \"cloudy\", \"rainy\", \"stormy\"]\r\n    return f\"The weather in {location} is {conditions[randint(0, 3)]} with a high of {randint(10, 30)}°C.\"\r\n\r\nasync def quick_start_tools() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        chat_client=chatClient,\r\n        name=\"MyAgent\",\r\n        instructions=\"You are a helpful agent.\",\r\n        tools=[get_weather],\r\n    )\r\n\r\n    result=await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\");\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n\r\n#### Multi-turn Conversation with Thread\r\nThread persistence across multiple conversations.\r\n\r\n``` python\r\nasync def quick_start_thread() -> None:\r\n    #...\r\n    agent=ChatAgent(\r\n        # settings...\r\n    )\r\n    # Create a new thread that will be reused\r\n    thread = agent.get_new_thread()\r\n\r\n    # First conversation\r\n    result1 = await agent.run(\"What's the weather like in Seattle?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result1.text}\") # The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n\r\n    # Second conversation using the same thread - maintains context\r\n    result2 = await agent.run(\"Pardon?\", thread=thread)\r\n    print(f\"Agent: {result2.text}\") # Sure. The weather in Seattle is rainy with a high of 18°C.\r\n```\r\n"
